// ===================== PIN (4 digits) =====================
const PIN_CODE = String(Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000));

// ===================== GAME STATE =====================
let gameState = 0;
let countdownStartStep = 0;
let countdownSeconds = 15;
let lockedRejoins = false;

let baseAnglesInitTime = 0;
let baseTracking = false;

// ===================== HELPERS =====================
function sanitizeName(name) {
    return (name || "Anonymous").replace(/[\[\]]/g, "|");
}

function getPlayerName(player) {
    return sanitizeName(player && player.name);
}

function ensureCustom(ship) {
    if (!ship.custom) ship.custom = {};
    if (ship.custom.hasBeenKicked === undefined) ship.custom.hasBeenKicked = false;
    return ship.custom;
}

function hideUI(ship, id) {
    try { ship.setUIComponent({ id: id, visible: false }); } catch (e) { }
}

function newLine() {
    game.modding.terminal.echo("\n");
}

function centeredEcho(text, style) {
    game.modding.terminal.echo(`${style}${text}]`);
}


function splitArgsQuoted(str) {
    str = String(str || "").trim();
    const out = [];
    let cur = "";
    let q = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
        const c = str[i];

        if (c === '"') { q = !q; continue; }

        if (!q && /\s/.test(c)) {
            if (cur.length) { out.push(cur); cur = ""; }
            continue;
        }
        cur += c;
    }
    if (cur.length) out.push(cur);
    return out;
}

// ===================== ALERT UI =====================
function alert(ship, line1 = "", line2 = "", color = "rgba(255,255,255,0.8)", time = 2500, warning = { v1: 4, v2: 4, h: 0 }) {
    ensureCustom(ship);
    clearTimeout(ship.custom.logtimeout);
    ship.custom.logtimeout = setTimeout(() => { hideUI(ship, "Text"); }, time);

    ship.setUIComponent({
        id: "Text",
        position: [-5, -5, 110, 110],
        clickable: false,
        visible: true,
        components: [
            { type: "text", position: [0, 18, 100, warning.v1], color: color, value: line1 },
            { type: "text", position: [0, 23 - warning.h, 100, warning.v2], color: color, value: line2 }
        ]
    });
}

// ===================== MODERATION =====================
function idle(who, showMessage = true) {
    const ship = game.ships[who];
    if (!ship) return;

    ensureCustom(ship);
    if (ship.custom.ISidle) {
        if (showMessage) game.modding.terminal.error(new Error(`${getPlayerName(ship)} is already frozen.`));
        return;
    }

    ship.set({ idle: true });
    ship.custom.ISidle = true;

    if (showMessage) {
        game.modding.terminal.echo(
            `[[g;#70ffc1;]\nPlayer ${getPlayerName(ship)} (index ${who}) has been frozen.]\n` +
            `[[gi;#ffa770;]INFO] Use unidle index to unfreeze the player.`
        );
    }
}

function unidle(who) {
    const ship = game.ships[who];
    if (!ship) return;

    ensureCustom(ship);
    if (!ship.custom.ISidle) {
        game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("This player is not frozen."));
        return;
    }

    ship.set({ idle: false });
    ship.custom.ISidle = false;
    game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#70ffc1;]\nPlayer ${getPlayerName(ship)} (index ${who}) has been unfrozen.]`);
}

function kickPlayer(ship, reason) {
    ensureCustom(ship);
    ship.custom.hasBeenKicked = true;
    ship.custom.kickReason = String(reason || "No reason");

    try { ship.set({ idle: true, collider: false, vx: 0, vy: 0 }); } catch (e) { }
    alert(ship, "KICKED", ship.custom.kickReason, "rgba(255,55,55,0.9)", 3000);

    setTimeout(() => { try { ship.set({ kill: true }); } catch (e) { } }, 150);
    setTimeout(() => { try { ship.set({ kill: true }); } catch (e) { } }, 800);
}

function ban(who, reason = "N/A") {
    const ship = game.ships[who];
    if (!ship) {
        game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("Index does not exist."));
        return;
    }

    ensureCustom(ship);
    const name = getPlayerName(ship);
    const shipIndex = game.ships.findIndex(s => s === ship);

    if (!gameOptions.BannedList.includes(name)) {
        gameOptions.BannedList.push(name);
        gameOptions.BannedListReasons.push(String(reason || "N/A"));
    }

    if (!ship.custom.ISidle) idle(shipIndex, false);

    setTimeout(() => { try { ship.set({ kill: true }); } catch (e) { } }, 10000);

    game.modding.terminal.echo(
        `[[g;#70ffc1;]\nPlayer: ${name} | index: ${shipIndex} has been banned.]\n` +
        `[[gi;#ffa770;]INFO] Use banlist to view bans.`
    );

    for (const otherShip of game.ships) {
        alert(otherShip, `Player banned: ${name}`, "", "rgba(255,55,55,0.8)");
        if (getPlayerName(otherShip) === name) {
            setTimeout(() => {
                alert(otherShip, "Warning!", "Your name matches a banned player.", "rgba(255,55,55,0.8)");
            }, 3500);
        }
    }
}

function unban(index) {
    index = parseInt(index, 10);
    if (!(index >= 0 && index < gameOptions.BannedList.length)) {
        game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("Invalid ban index (or already unbanned)."));
        return;
    }

    const name = gameOptions.BannedList[index];
    const reason = gameOptions.BannedListReasons[index];

    gameOptions.BannedList.splice(index, 1);
    gameOptions.BannedListReasons.splice(index, 1);

    for (const ship of game.ships) {
        alert(ship, `Unbanned: ${name}`, `Reason was: ${reason}`, "rgba(55,255,55,0.8)", 2500);
    }
    game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#70ffc1;]\nPlayer: ${name} has been unbanned.]`);
}

function checkBanned(ship) {
    if (!ship) return;
    ensureCustom(ship);

    const name = getPlayerName(ship);
    if (gameOptions.BannedList.includes(name)) {
        const idx = game.ships.indexOf(ship);
        if (!ship.custom.ISidle) idle(idx, false);
        kickPlayer(ship, "Banned player");
    }
}

function spectate(index) {
  let ship = game.ships[index];
  if (ship.type != 102) {
    ship.set({type: 102, collider: false});
    ship.custom.spectating = true;
  }
  else {
    ship.set({ type: 101, collider: true, shield: 100}); 
    ship.custom.spectating = false;
  }
}


// ===================== MAP OPEN =====================
function MapOpen(tm = 0) {
    if (!game.custom) game.custom = {};

    if (!game.custom.modeIsRuned) {
        setTimeout(function () {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(
                `\n\n[[gb;#007bff;]★ ${gameOptions.Name} ★]\n\n` +
                `[[gi;#00d5ff;]Admin panel loaded.]\n` +
                `[[gi;#00d5ff;]MapID=${RANDOM_MAP_ID} MapSize=${RANDOM_MAP_SIZE}]\n`
            );
        }, tm);

        setTimeout(function () {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(
                `[[gu;#00d5ff;]Version] [[gb;#ffc300;]${gameOptions.Version}]\n` +
                `[[gu;#00d5ff;]Support] [[gb;#ffc300;]${gameOptions.Support[0]}]\n`
            );
        }, tm += 200);

        setTimeout(function () {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(
                `\n[[gu;#ffdf00;]Links]\n${gameOptions.Connexions.discord}\n${gameOptions.Connexions.documentation}\n`
            );
            game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#ffdf00;]PIN CODE: ${PIN_CODE}]`);
            game.modding.terminal.echo("https://starblast.io/#" + game.modding.address);
        }, tm += 250);

        game.custom.modeIsRuned = true;
    }
}

// ===================== SAY + STARTMODE UI =====================
function parseSayArgs(parts) {
    const args = parts.slice(1);
    if (!args.length) return null;

    const joined = args.join(" ");
    const m = joined.match(/\s*(.*?)(?:\s+(\d{1,3}))?(?:\s+(.+))?\s*$/);

    let text = (m && m[1] ? m[1] : "").trim();
    if (!text) return null;

    let duration = (m && m[2] !== undefined) ? parseInt(m[2], 10) : 4;
    let color = (m && m[3] !== undefined) ? String(m[3]).trim() : "#FFFFFF";

    duration = Math.max(1, Math.min(30, isNaN(duration) ? 4 : duration));
    if (!color) color = "#FFFFFF";

    if (/^#([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/i.test(color)) return { text, duration, color };
    if (/^(rgb|rgba|hsl|hsla)\(\s*[^)]+\s*\)$/i.test(color)) return { text, duration, color };
    return { text, duration, color };
}

function parseAnnounceArgs(parts) {
    const args = parts.slice(1);
    if (!args.length) return null;

    const modeRaw = String(args[0] || "banner").toLowerCase();
    const mode = (modeRaw === "system") ? "system" : "banner";

    const rest = args.slice(1);
    if (!rest.length) return null;

    const parsed = parseSayArgs(["say", ...rest]);
    if (!parsed) return null;

    return { mode, text: parsed.text, duration: parsed.duration, color: parsed.color };
}

function showAnnouncement(ship, mode, text, durationSec, color) {
    const id = (mode === "system") ? "SYS_ANNOUNCE" : "BANNER_ANNOUNCE";
    const pos = (mode === "system") ? [5, 6, 90, 12] : [15, 40, 70, 20];

    ensureCustom(ship);
    clearTimeout(ship.custom.__say_to);

    ship.setUIComponent({
        id,
        position: pos,
        clickable: false,
        visible: true,
        components: [
            { type: "box", position: [0, 0, 100, 100], fill: "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)" },
            { type: "text", position: [5, 18, 90, 64], align: "center", value: text, color: color || "#FFFFFF" }
        ]
    });

    ship.custom.__say_to = setTimeout(() => {
        try { ship.setUIComponent({ id, visible: false }); } catch (e) { }
    }, durationSec * 1000);
}

function showAnnouncementToAll(text, durationSec, color, mode) {
    for (const ship of game.ships) showAnnouncement(ship, mode || "banner", text, durationSec, color);
}

function renderWaitingUI(ship, secondsLeft) {
    ship.setUIComponent({
        id: "WAIT_MODE",
        position: [12, 35, 76, 30],
        clickable: false,
        visible: true,
        components: [
            { type: "box", position: [0, 0, 100, 100], fill: "rgba(0,0,0,0.65)" },
            { type: "text", position: [0, 18, 100, 28], align: "center", value: "WAITING FOR ADMIN TO START", color: "#FFFFFF" },
            { type: "text", position: [0, 55, 100, 22], align: "center", value: (secondsLeft > 0 ? `STARTS IN ${secondsLeft}s` : "STARTING..."), color: "#FFFF00" }
        ]
    });
}

function hideWaitingUI(ship) {
    try { ship.setUIComponent({ id: "WAIT_MODE", visible: false }); } catch (e) { }
}

function tickGameState(game) {
    if (gameState === 0) {
        for (const ship of game.ships) {
            ensureCustom(ship);
            if (ship.custom.passedPin && game.step % 60 === 0) renderWaitingUI(ship, 0);
        }
        return;
    }

    if (gameState === 1) {
        const elapsedSec = Math.floor((game.step - countdownStartStep) / 60);
        const left = Math.max(0, countdownSeconds - elapsedSec);

        for (const ship of game.ships) {
            ensureCustom(ship);
            if (ship.custom.passedPin && game.step % 10 === 0) renderWaitingUI(ship, left);
        }

        if (left === 0) {
            gameState = 2;

            for (const ship of game.ships) hideWaitingUI(ship);
            showAnnouncementToAll("MODE STARTED", 3, "#55FF55", "system");

            if (!game.custom) game.custom = {};
            game.custom.waveIdx = null;

            for (const ship of game.ships) {
                ship.set({ idle: false, collider: true });
            }
        }
    }
}



// ===================== COMMANDS (ONCE) =====================
function installCommandsOnce() {
    if (!game.custom) game.custom = {};
    if (game.custom._commandsInstalled) return;
    game.custom._commandsInstalled = true;

    game.modding.commands.info = function (req) {
        const totalPlayers = game.ships.length;
        const bannedCount = gameOptions.BannedList.length;
        const bannedPlayers = bannedCount === 0 ? "None" : gameOptions.BannedList.join(", ");

        const wave = (typeof getWaveInfo === "function") ? getWaveInfo(game) : null;
        const waveLeftSec = wave ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(wave.endsInTicks / 60)) : 0;
        const waveMM = String(Math.floor(waveLeftSec / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
        const waveSS = String(waveLeftSec % 60).padStart(2, "0");

        let modeText = (gameState === 0) ? "WAITING" : (gameState === 1) ? "COUNTDOWN" : "STARTED";
        let countdownLeft = "";
        if (gameState === 1) {
            const elapsedSec = Math.floor((game.step - countdownStartStep) / 60);
            const left = Math.max(0, countdownSeconds - elapsedSec);
            countdownLeft = ` | T-${left}s`;
        }

        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[gb;#00d5ff;]★ ${gameOptions.Name} ★]`);
        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#70ffc1;]Players] [[gb;#fffc70;]${totalPlayers}] [[g;#ff7070;]Bans] [[gb;#fffc70;]${bannedCount}]`);
        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#ffdf00;]PIN] [[gb;#fffc70;]${PIN_CODE}]`);
        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#70aeff;]Map] [[gb;#fffc70;]ID=${RANDOM_MAP_ID}] [[g;#70aeff;]Size] [[gb;#fffc70;]${RANDOM_MAP_SIZE}]`);
        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#ffb000;]Mode] [[gb;#fffc70;]${modeText}${countdownLeft}]`);

        if (wave) {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#c9a6ff;]Wave] [[gb;#fffc70;]${wave.idx} (${wave.name})] [[g;#c9a6ff;]TimeLeft] [[gb;#fffc70;]${waveMM}:${waveSS}]`);
        }

        if (bannedCount > 0) {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#ff7070;]Banned players] [[gb;#fffc70;]${bannedPlayers}]`);
        }

        game.modding.terminal.echo("\n[[gu;#70aeff;]Players list]");
        game.modding.terminal.echo("[[g;#9ad1ff;]idx | name | team(hue) | type | alive | idle | crystals | shield/gen | score]");

        for (let i = 0; i < totalPlayers; i++) {
            const p = game.ships[i];

            const name = getPlayerName(p);
            const type = p.type;
            const team = p.team;

            let hue = "?";
            try {
                if (game.options && game.options.teams && game.options.teams[team]) hue = game.options.teams[team].hue;
            } catch (e) { }

            const alive = p.alive ? "Y" : "N";
            const idleFlag = p.idle ? "Y" : "N";

            const crystals = (p.crystals == null) ? 0 : Math.round(p.crystals);
            const shield = (p.shield == null) ? 0 : Math.round(p.shield);
            const gen = (p.generator == null) ? 0 : Math.round(p.generator);
            const score = (p.score == null) ? 0 : Math.round(p.score);

            const x = Math.round(p.x || 0);
            const y = Math.round(p.y || 0);
            const vx = Math.round(p.vx || 0);
            const vy = Math.round(p.vy || 0);

            game.modding.terminal.echo(
                `[[g;#fffc70;]${i}] [[g;#ffffff;]${name}] [[g;#70ffc1;]${team}(${hue})] ` +
                `[[g;#ffdf00;]${type}] [[g;#85ff70;]${alive}] [[g;#85ff70;]${idleFlag}] ` +
                `[[g;#70aeff;]${crystals}] [[g;#ff7070;]${shield}/${gen}] [[g;#c9a6ff;]${score}] `
            );
        }

        game.modding.terminal.echo("\n");
    };
    
    game.modding.commands.infoTeams = function (req) {
      let numTeams = gameOptions.NumberOfTeams
      for (let i = 0; i < numTeams; i++) {
        let players = 0;
        let verified = 0;
        let totalScore = 0;
        for (const ship of game.ships) {
          if (ship.team === i) {
            players++;
            totalScore += ship.score;
            if (!ship.custom.awaitingManualVerification && ship.custom.passedPin && !ship.custom.spectating) {
              verified++;
            }
          }
        }
        let hue = game.options.teams[i].hue;
        let teamColor;
        if (hue === 0) teamColor = "Red";
        else if (hue === 120) teamColor = "Green";
        else if (hue === 240) teamColor = "Blue";
        game.modding.terminal.echo("Team: " + teamColor + "  Players: " + players + "  Verified: " + verified + "  Total Score: " + totalScore);
      }
    }
    
    game.modding.commands.spec = function (req) {
      const parts = String(req).trim().split(" ");
      const index = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
      
      if (isNaN(index) || index < 0 || index >= game.ships.length) {
        game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("Invalid player index"));
        return;
      }
      spectate(index);
    }

    game.modding.commands.ban = function (req) {
        const parts = String(req).trim().split(" ");
        const index = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
        const reason = parts.slice(2).join(" ") || "N/A";

        if (isNaN(index) || index < 0 || index >= game.ships.length) {
            game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("Invalid player index"));
            return;
        }

        ban(index, reason);
    };

    game.modding.commands.banlist = function (req) {
        if (!(gameOptions.BannedList.length > 0)) {
            game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("There are no banned players in this game."));
            return;
        }
        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#70aeff;]\nBanned players list:]\n`);
        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#689bdd;]Count: ${gameOptions.BannedList.length}]\n`);
        gameOptions.BannedList.forEach((player, i) => {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#70e4ff;]Index: ${i}, Name: ${player}, Reason: ${gameOptions.BannedListReasons[i]}]`);
        });
        game.modding.terminal.echo(`\n[[gi;#ffa770;]INFO] Use unban(index) to unban.\n`);
    };

    game.modding.commands.unban = function (req) {
        const parts = String(req).trim().split(" ");
        const index = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
        unban(index);
    };

    game.modding.commands.idle = function (req) {
        const parts = String(req).trim().split(" ");
        const index = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
        if (isNaN(index) || index < 0 || index >= game.ships.length) {
            game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("Invalid player index"));
            return;
        }
        idle(index, true);
    };

    game.modding.commands.unidle = function (req) {b
        const parts = String(req).trim().split(" ");
        const index = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
        if (isNaN(index) || index < 0 || index >= game.ships.length) {
            game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("Invalid player index"));
            return;
        }
        unidle(index);
    };

    game.modding.commands.kick = function (req) {
        const parts = String(req).trim().split(" ");
        const index = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
        const reason = parts.slice(2).join(" ") || "No reason";

        if (isNaN(index) || index < 0 || index >= game.ships.length) {
            game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("Invalid player index"));
            return;
        }

        const ship = game.ships[index];
        kickPlayer(ship, reason);
        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#ff7070;]${getPlayerName(ship)} was kicked | reason: ${reason}]`);
    };

    game.modding.commands.say = function (req) {
        const parts = splitArgsQuoted(req);
        const parsed = parseSayArgs(parts);

        if (!parsed) {
            game.modding.terminal.error(new Error('Usage: say "<message>" [duration] [color]'));
            return;
        }

        showAnnouncementToAll(parsed.text, parsed.duration, parsed.color, "banner");
        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#70ffc1;]\nText sent: "${parsed.text}" | Duration: ${parsed.duration}s | Color: ${parsed.color}]`);
    };

    game.modding.commands.announce = function (req) {
        const parts = splitArgsQuoted(req);
        const parsed = parseAnnounceArgs(parts);

        if (!parsed) {
            game.modding.terminal.error(
                new Error('Usage: announce <banner|system> "<text>" [duration] [color]')
            );
            return;
        }

        showAnnouncementToAll(parsed.text, parsed.duration, parsed.color, parsed.mode);
        game.modding.terminal.echo(
            `[[g;#70ffc1;]\nAnnouncement sent: "${parsed.text}" | Mode: ${parsed.mode} | Duration: ${parsed.duration}s | Color: ${parsed.color}]`
        );
    };

    game.modding.commands.startmode = function (req) {
        const parts = String(req || "").trim().split(" ");
        const sec = parseInt(parts[1] || "15", 10);
        const s = Math.max(3, Math.min(60, isNaN(sec) ? 15 : sec));

        if (gameState !== 0) return "Game already started or in progress!";

        gameState = 1;
        countdownSeconds = s;
        countdownStartStep = game.step;

        newLine();
        centeredEcho("⏳ COUNTDOWN STARTED ⏳", "[[gb;#FFFF00;]");
        centeredEcho(`${countdownSeconds} SECONDS`, "[[b;#FFFF00;]");
        newLine();

        showAnnouncementToAll(`Countdown started: ${countdownSeconds}s`, 3, "#FFFF00", "system");
        gameOptions.RespawnTimers.startmodeTime = game.step;
        return "Countdown Started!";
    };
    
    game.modding.commands.initBase = function (req) {
      getInitBaseAngles();
    }
    
    game.modding.commands.unlockRejoins = function (req) {
        lockedRejoins = false;
        game.modding.terminal.echo('[[g;#ffffff;]Switching to PIN verification]');
    }
    
    game.modding.commands.lockRejoins = function (req) {
        lockedRejoins = true;
        game.modding.terminal.echo('[[g;#ffffff;]Switching to manual verification]');
    }
    
    game.modding.commands.verify = function (req) {
        const parts = String(req).trim().split(" ");
        const index = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
        if (isNaN(index) || index < 0 || index >= game.ships.length) {
            game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("Invalid player index"));
            return;
        }
        verify(index);
    }
    
    game.modding.commands.unverify = function (req) {
      const parts = String(req).trim().split(" ");
      const index = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
      if (isNaN(index) || index < 0 || index >= game.ships.length) {
          game.modding.terminal.error(new Error("Invalid player index"));
          return;
      }
      unverify(index);
    }

    game.modding.commands.help = function (req) {
        const parts = splitArgsQuoted(req);
        const topic = String(parts[1] || "").toLowerCase();

        const linesMain = [
            "[[gb;#00d5ff;]★ Commands ★]",
            '[[g;#70aeff;]help ["cmd"]] [[g;#ffffff;]- Show this list / command details]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]info] [[g;#ffffff;]- Players, bans, mode info]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]infoTeams] [[g;#ffffff;]- Numbers, scores info]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]ban <index> ["reason..."]] [[g;#ffffff;]- Ban player by index]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]banlist] [[g;#ffffff;]- Show banned list]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]unban <banIndex>] [[g;#ffffff;]- Unban by banlist index]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]idle <index>] [[g;#ffffff;]- Freeze player]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]unidle <index>] [[g;#ffffff;]- Unfreeze player]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]kick <index> ["reason..."]] [[g;#ffffff;]- Kick player]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]say "<text>" [duration] [color]] [[g;#ffffff;]- Banner to all]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]announce <banner|system> "<text>" [duration] [color]] [[g;#ffffff;]- Announcement to all]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]startmode [seconds]] [[g;#ffffff;]- Start countdown]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]lockRejoins] [[g;#ffffff;]- Switch to manual verification]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]unlockRejoins] [[g;#ffffff;]- Switch to PIN verification]',
            '[[g;#70aeff;]verify <index> [[g;#ffffff;]- Verify player by index]',
            //todo: add help for initbase, spec
            
        ];

        if (!topic) {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(linesMain.join("\n") + "\n");
            return "OK";
        }

        if (topic === "say") {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(
                [
                    "[[gb;#00d5ff;]help: say]",
                    'Usage: say "<text>" [duration] [color]',
                    'Example: say "Hello world" 5 "#00ffcc"',
                ].join("\n") + "\n"
            );
            return "OK";
        }

        if (topic === "announce") {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(
                [
                    "[[gb;#00d5ff;]help: announce]",
                    'Usage: announce <banner|system> "<text>" [duration] [color]',
                    'Example: announce system "MODE STARTED" 3 "#55FF55"',
                ].join("\n") + "\n"
            );
            return "OK";
        }

        if (topic === "ban") {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(
                [
                    "[[gb;#00d5ff;]help: ban]",
                    'Usage: ban <index> "reason..."',
                    'Example: ban 3 "toxic"',
                ].join("\n") + "\n"
            );
            return "OK";
        }

        if (topic === "kick") {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(
                [
                    "[[gb;#00d5ff;]help: kick]",
                    'Usage: kick <index> "reason..."',
                    'Example: kick 3 "AFK"',
                ].join("\n") + "\n"
            );
            return "OK";
        }

        if (topic === "startmode") {
            game.modding.terminal.echo(
                [
                    "[[gb;#00d5ff;]help: startmode]",
                    "Usage: startmode [seconds]",
                    "Example: startmode 15",
                ].join("\n") + "\n"
            );
            return "OK";
        }

        game.modding.terminal.echo(`[[g;#ff7070;]Unknown help topic: ${topic}]\n`);
        return "OK";
    };
}

// ===================== PIN UI =====================
const PinUI = {
    ids: [
        "PIN_BG", "PIN_PANEL", "PIN_TITLE", "PIN_DISPLAY",
        "PIN_OK", "PIN_BS",
        "PIN_0", "PIN_1", "PIN_2", "PIN_3", "PIN_4", "PIN_5", "PIN_6", "PIN_7", "PIN_8", "PIN_9"
    ],

    hideAll(ship) {
        for (const id of this.ids) {
            try { ship.setUIComponent({ id: id, visible: false }); } catch (e) { }
        }
    },

    renderDisplay(ship) {
        const s = pinSessions.get(ship.id) || { input: "" };
        const digits = String(s.input || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 4);
        const masked = (digits + "____").slice(0, 4).split("").join(" ");

        ship.setUIComponent({
            id: "PIN_DISPLAY",
            position: [30, 28, 40, 8],
            clickable: false,
            visible: true,
            components: [
                { type: "text", position: [0, 10, 100, 80], align: "center", value: masked, color: "#ffffff" }
            ]
        });
    },

    show(ship) {
        ensureCustom(ship);
        if (ship.custom.passedPin) return;
        setTimeout(() => {
            ship.set({ idle: true, collider: false, vx: 0, vy: 0 });
        }, 500);

        if (!pinSessions.has(ship.id)) {
            pinSessions.set(ship.id, {
                input: "",
                tries: 0,
                failed: false,
                expected: PIN_CODE
            });

            setTimeout(() => {
                const s = pinSessions.get(ship.id);
                if (!s || ship.custom.passedPin || s.failed) return;
                s.failed = true;
                kickPlayer(ship, "PIN timeout");
            }, 20000);
        }

        ship.setUIComponent({
            id: "PIN_TITLE",
            position: [20, 18, 60, 10],
            clickable: false,
            visible: true,
            components: [
                { type: "text", position: [0, 18, 100, 60], align: "center", value: "Enter 4-digit code", color: "#ffffff" }
            ]
        });

        this.renderDisplay(ship);

        const buttons = [
            ["1", 29, 38], ["2", 44, 38], ["3", 59, 38],
            ["4", 29, 50], ["5", 44, 50], ["6", 59, 50],
            ["7", 29, 62], ["8", 44, 62], ["9", 59, 62],
            ["BS", 29, 74], ["0", 44, 74], ["OK", 59, 74],
        ];

        for (const [label, x, y] of buttons) {
            const id =
                label === "OK" ? "PIN_OK" :
                    label === "BS" ? "PIN_BS" :
                        "PIN_" + label;

            const fill = (label === "OK") ? "#ffb000" : "#2b2b2b";
            const textColor = (label === "OK") ? "#000000" : "#ffffff";
            const displayLabel = (label === "BS") ? "⌫" : label;

            ship.setUIComponent({
                id,
                position: [x, y, 12, 10],
                clickable: true,
                visible: true,
                components: [
                    { type: "box", position: [0, 0, 100, 100], fill: fill },
                    { type: "text", position: [0, 20, 100, 60], align: "center", value: displayLabel, color: textColor }
                ]
            });
        }

        ship.setUIComponent({
            id: "PIN_PANEL",
            position: [20, 16, 60, 72],
            clickable: false,
            visible: true,
            components: [
                { type: "box", position: [0, 0, 100, 100], fill: "rgba(18,18,18,1)" }
            ]
        });

        ship.setUIComponent({
            id: "PIN_BG",
            position: [0, 0, 100, 100],
            clickable: false,
            visible: true,
            components: [
                { type: "box", position: [0, 0, 100, 100], fill: "black" }
            ]
        });
    },

    pressDigit(ship, digit) {
        const s = pinSessions.get(ship.id);
        if (!s || ship.custom.passedPin || s.failed) return;

        digit = String(digit);
        if (!/^[0-9]$/.test(digit)) return;

        s.input = String(s.input || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
        if (s.input.length >= 4) return;

        s.input = (s.input + digit).slice(0, 4);
        this.renderDisplay(ship);
    },

    backspace(ship) {
        const s = pinSessions.get(ship.id);
        if (!s || ship.custom.passedPin || s.failed) return;

        s.input = String(s.input || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
        s.input = s.input.slice(0, -1);
        this.renderDisplay(ship);
    },

    submit(ship) {
        const s = pinSessions.get(ship.id);
        if (!s || ship.custom.passedPin || s.failed) return;

        const entered = String(s.input || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 4);
        const expected = String(s.expected || PIN_CODE).replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 4);

        if (entered.length !== 4) {
            alert(ship, "ERROR", "Enter 4 digits", "rgba(255,120,0,0.9)", 1200);
            return;
        }

        if (entered !== expected) {
            alert(ship, "WRONG CODE", "You were kicked", "rgba(255,55,55,0.9)", 1500);
            s.failed = true;
            kickPlayer(ship, "Wrong PIN code");
            return;
        }

        ship.custom.passedPin = true;
        
        if (lockedRejoins) {
          ship.custom.awaitingManualVerification = true;
          this.hideAll(ship);
          ship.instructorSays("Awaiting Verification By Host");
        }
        else {
          this.hideAll(ship);
          if (gameState !== 2) {
              ship.set({ idle: true, collider: false, vx: 0, vy: 0 });
          } else {
              ship.set({ idle: false, collider: true });
          }
          alert(ship, "ACCESS GRANTED", "", "rgba(55,255,55,0.9)", 1200);

        if (!ship.custom.init) initShip(ship);
          
        }
    }
};

function verify(who) {
  const ship = game.ships[who];
  
  if (!ship.custom.passedPin) {
    game.modding.terminal.echo('[[g;#ffffff;]Invalid target: Target has not entered PIN');
    return;
  }
  
  if (!ship.custom.awaitingManualVerification) {
    game.modding.terminal.echo('[[g;#ffffff;]Invalid target: Target is already verified');
    return
  }
  
  ship.custom.awaitingManualVerification = false;
  PinUI.hideAll(ship);
  if (gameState !== 2) {
    ship.set({ idle: true, collider: false, vx: 0, vy: 0 });
  } else {
    ship.set({ idle: false, collider: true });
  }
  ship.hideInstructor();
  alert(ship, "ACCESS GRANTED", "", "rgba(55,255,55,0.9)", 1200);
  game.modding.terminal.echo('[[g;#ffffff;]Verified successfully');
}

function unverify(who) {
  const ship = game.ships[who];
  
  if (!ship.custom.passedPin) {
    game.modding.terminal.echo('[[g;#ffffff;]Invalid target: Target has not entered PIN');
    return;
  }
  
  if (ship.custom.awaitingManualVerification) {
    game.modding.terminal.echo('[[g;#ffffff;]Invalid target: Target is already unverified');
    return
  }
  
  ship.custom.awaitingManualVerification = true;
  ship.set({idle: true, collider: false, vx: 0, vy: 0});
  ship.instructorSays("Unverified by host. Awaiting manual verification.");
  game.modding.terminal.echo('[[g;#ffffff;]Unverified successfully');
}

// ===================== PLAYER INIT / RESPAWN =====================
function initShip(ship) {
    ensureCustom(ship);
    if (!ship.custom.init) ship.custom.init = true;
    game.modding.terminal.echo(`\n[[g;#70ffc1;]New player\nIndex: ${game.ships.indexOf(ship)}, Name: ${getPlayerName(ship)}]`);
}

function respawnShip(ship) {
  //give gems
  if (gameOptions.GiveCatchupGemsOnSpawn) {
    let team = ship.team;
    let allyScore = 0;
    let opScore = 0;
    for (const p of game.ships) {
      if (p.team == team) allyScore += p.score; else opScore += p.score;
    }
    let ratio = 1 - (allyScore / opScore) - 0.1;
    if (ratio <= 0) return;
    if (ratio > 0.35) ratio = 0.35;
    let tier = Math.floor(ship.type / 100);
    let gem = Math.round(20 * tier**2 * ratio);
    ship.set({crystals: gem})
  }
}

// ===================== CONNECTION TRACKER =====================
let connectedShips = new Map();
this.eventTriggered = false;

// ===================== BASE TRACKING ============= //

function getInitBaseAngles() {
  while (initBaseAngles.length < gameOptions.NumberOfTeams) {
    for (const ship of game.ships) {
      if (ship.team == initBaseAngles.length) {
        let angle = distance({x: 0, y: 0}, {x: ship.x, y: ship.y}).angle;
        initBaseAngles.push(angle);
      }
    }
  }
  echo(game.ships[0].x)
  echo(game.ships[0].y)
  
  baseAnglesInitTime = game.step;
  baseTracking = true;
  echo(getBaseXY(game.ships[0].team).X)
  echo(getBaseXY(game.ships[0].team).Y);
}

function getBaseXY(base) {
  let phase = initBaseAngles[base];
  let radius = (Math.sqrt(2)/2) * CUSTOM_MAP_SIZE * 5;
  let steps = game.step - baseAnglesInitTime;
  let theta = steps / 60 / 3600 % 1 * Math.PI * 2;
  let x = radius * Math.cos(theta + phase);
  let y = (radius * Math.sin(theta + phase));
  return {X: x, Y: y};
}

function getClosestBasePair() {
  let closest = {
    base1: -1,
    base2: -1,
    distance: Infinity
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < gameOptions.NumberOfTeams - 1; i++) {
    const pos1 = getBaseXY(i);

    for (let j = i + 1; j < gameOptions.NumberOfTeams; j++) {
      const pos2 = getBaseXY(j);

      const d = distance(
        { x: pos1.X, y: pos1.Y },
        { x: pos2.X, y: pos2.Y }
      ).distance;

      if (d < closest.distance) {
        closest = {
          base1: i,
          base2: j,
          distance: d
        };
      }
    }
  }

  return closest;
}

const taskUIs = {
  target(team) {
    let hue;
    let text;
    if (team === 0) {
      hue = "#ff0000";
      text = "red";
    }
    else if (team === 1) {
      hue = "#0015ff";
      text = "blue";
    }
    else if (team === 2) {
      hue = "#00ff44";
      text = "green";
    }
    
    return {
      id:"target",
      position: [60,5,10,5],
      clickable: false,
      visible: true,
      components: [{type: "text", position: [0,0,100,100], value: "Target: " + text, color: hue}]
    }
  },
  noTarget: {
    id: "target",
    position: [60, 5, 10, 5],
    clickable: false,
    visible: true,
    components: [{type: "text", position: [0,0,100,100], value: "No Target - Mine Base", color: "#a8a8a8"}]
  },
}

function distance (origin = {x: 0, y: 0}, target = {x: 0, y: 0}) {
	const mapsize_xy = (game.options || this.options).map_size * 10;
		
	const x_diff = target.x - origin.x;
	const y_diff = target.y - origin.y;

	const x_plus_mapsize = x_diff + mapsize_xy;
		const x_minus_mapsize = x_diff - mapsize_xy;

	const y_plus_mapsize = y_diff + mapsize_xy;
	const y_minus_mapsize = y_diff - mapsize_xy;

	const d = [
		[x_diff, y_diff],
		[x_diff, y_plus_mapsize],
		[x_diff, y_minus_mapsize],
		[x_plus_mapsize, y_diff],
		[x_minus_mapsize, y_diff],
		[x_plus_mapsize, y_plus_mapsize],
		[x_plus_mapsize, y_minus_mapsize],
		[x_minus_mapsize, y_plus_mapsize],
		[x_minus_mapsize, y_minus_mapsize]
	];

	let shortestPath, shortestAngle;
	for (let point of d) {
		let path = Math.sqrt(point[0] ** 2 + point[1] ** 2);
		let angle = Math.atan2(point[1], point[0]);

		if (shortestPath == null || shortestPath > path) {
			shortestPath = path;
			shortestAngle = angle;
		}
	}

	return {
		distance: shortestPath,
		angle: shortestAngle
	}
}

function applyTargetUI(base1, base2) {
  for (const ship of game.ships) {
    if (ship.team === base1 && ship.custom.targetUI != base2) {
      let UI = taskUIs.target(base2);
      ship.setUIComponent(UI);
      ship.custom.targetUI = base2;
    }
    else if (ship.team === base2 && ship.custom.targetUI != base1) {
      let UI = taskUIs.target(base1);
      ship.setUIComponent(UI);
      ship.custom.targetUI = base1;
    }
    else if (ship.team != base1 && ship.team != base2) {
      let UI = taskUIs.noTarget;
      ship.setUIComponent(UI);
      ship.custom.targetUI = -1;
    }
  }
}

function removeShipsFromNonTargetBase() {
  for (const ship of game.ships) {
    let allowedBases = [ship.team, ship.custom.targetUI];
    for (let i = 0; i < gameOptions.NumberOfTeams; i++) {
      if (i != allowedBases[0] && i != allowedBases[1]) {
        let basePos = getBaseXY(i);
        let baseX = basePos.X;
        let baseY = basePos.Y;
        let dist = distance({x: baseX, y: baseY}, {x: ship.x, y: ship.y}).distance;
        if (dist < 100) {
          basePos = getBaseXY(ship.team);
          ship.set({x: basePos.X, y: basePos.Y});
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

function tickBaseTracking() {
  if (game.step % 60 === 0 && baseTracking) {
    let closest = getClosestBasePair();
    applyTargetUI(closest.base1, closest.base2);
    removeShipsFromNonTargetBase(closest.base1, closest.base2);
  }
}

// =================== RESPAWN TIMERS ================== //

function startRespawnTimer(ship) {
  echo("started respawn timer for: " + ship.name)
  ensureRespawnTimer(ship);
  ship.custom.respawnTimer.active = true;
  let totalTicks = ship.custom.respawnTimer.totalTicks
  let newTotalTicks = gameOptions.RespawnTimers.change(totalTicks);
  ship.custom.respawnTimer.totalTicks = newTotalTicks
  ship.custom.respawnTimer.ticksLeft = newTotalTicks
}

function ensureRespawnTimer(ship) {
  if (!ship.custom.respawnTimer) {
    
    ship.custom.respawnTimer = {
      active: false,
      totalTicks: gameOptions.RespawnTimers.start,
      ticksLeft: 0,
    }
  }
}

function sendRespawnTimerUI(ship) {
  let time = Math.round(ship.custom.respawnTimer.ticksLeft / 60)
  ship.setUIComponent({
    id: "respawnTimer",
    position: [45, 25, 10, 10],
    visible: true,
    components: [
        {
            type: "text",
            position: [0, 0, 100, 100],
            color: "#FFFFFF",
            value: String(time),
        }
    ]
  });
}

function hideRespawnTimerUI(ship) {
  ship.setUIComponent({
    id: "respawnTimer",
    position: [-1, 0, 0, 0],
  })
}

function finishedRespawnTimer(ship) {
  ship.custom.respawnTimer.active = false;
  hideRespawnTimerUI(ship);
  ship.set({idle: false, collider: true, invulnerable: 900, generator: 900});
}


function disableShipsOnTimer() {
  if (game.step % 6 != 0) return;
  for (const ship of game.ships) {
    if (ship.custom.respawnTimer.active) {
      ship.set({idle: true, collider: false, vx: 0, vy: 0, generator: 0});
    }
  }
}

function decrementRespawnTimers() {
  
  if (game.step % 60 != 0) return;
  for (const ship of game.ships) {
    if (ship.custom.respawnTimer.active) {
      ship.custom.respawnTimer.ticksLeft -= 60
      
      sendRespawnTimerUI(ship)
      
      if (ship.custom.respawnTimer.ticksLeft <= 0) finishedRespawnTimer(ship);
    }
  }
}

function tickRespawnTimers() {
  if (!gameOptions.RespawnTimers.enabled) return;

  if (game.step - gameOptions.RespawnTimers.startmodeTime === gameOptions.RespawnTimers.threshold) {
    gameOptions.RespawnTimers.active = true;
    showAnnouncementToAll("Death Timers Enabled", 4, "#FFFFFF", "system")
    echo("timers active")
  }
  
  if (gameOptions.RespawnTimers.active) {
    disableShipsOnTimer();
    decrementRespawnTimers();
  }
}

function tickDenyT7Healing() {
  if (!gameOptions.DenyT7Healing) return;
  if (game.step % 60 != 0) return;
  for (const ship of game.ships) {
    let tier = Math.floor(ship.type / 100);
    let isHealing = ship.healing;
    if (tier == 7 && isHealing) {
      ship.set({healing: false})
    }
  }
}


// ===================== TICK =====================
this.tick = function (game) {
    if (!game.custom) game.custom = {};
    if (!game.custom._boot) {
        game.custom._boot = true;
        MapOpen();
    }

    installCommandsOnce();

    tickGameState(game);
    
    if (gameState == 0 && game.step % 120 === 0) { //makes sure even those who pass PIN quickly get idled
      for (const ship of game.ships) {
        ship.set({idle: true, vx: 0, vy: 0});
      }
    }

    for (const ship of game.ships) {
        ensureCustom(ship);

        if (game.step % 60 === 0) {
          
          if (!ship.custom.passedPin) {
            ship.set({idle: true, collilder: false, vx: 0, vy: 0});
          }
          
          if (ship.custom.awaitingManualVerification) {
            ship.set({idle: true, collider: false, vx: 0, vy: 0});
          }
          
          if (ship.custom.spectating) {
            ship.set({collider: false})
          }
        }
        

        if (ship.custom.alive !== ship.alive) {
            ship.custom.alive = ship.alive;
            if (ship.alive) {
                try { this.event({ name: "ship_spawned", ship: ship }, game); } catch (e) { }
            }
        }

        connectedShips.set(ship.id, ship);
    }

    for (const [id, ship] of connectedShips) {
        if (!game.ships.includes(ship)) {
            try { this.event({ name: "ship_disconnected", ship: ship }, game); } catch (e) { }
            connectedShips.delete(id);
        }
    }

    //checks for team win
    if (game.step >= (10 * 3600) && game.step % 60 === 0) {
        const aliveShips = game.ships.filter(s => s.alive);
        const uniqueTeams = [...new Set(aliveShips.map(s => s.team))];
        if (uniqueTeams.length === 1 && aliveShips.length > 0 && !this.eventTriggered) {
            this.eventTriggered = true;
            try { this.event({ name: "team_win", ships: aliveShips }, game); } catch (e) { }
        }
    }
    
    tickBaseTracking();
    
    tickRespawnTimers();
    
    tickDenyT7Healing();
};

// ===================== EVENT DISPATCH =====================
this.event = function (event, game) {
    switch (event.name) {
        case "ui_component_clicked": {
            if (typeof event.id === "string" && event.id.startsWith("PIN_")) {
                const ship = event.ship;

                if (event.id === "PIN_OK") return PinUI.submit(ship);
                if (event.id === "PIN_BS") return PinUI.backspace(ship);

                const d = event.id.slice(4);
                if (/^[0-9]$/.test(d)) return PinUI.pressDigit(ship, d);
                return;
            }
            
            if (event.id === "using_subspace" && !event.ship.custom.usingSubspace) {
              event.ship.custom.usingSubspace = true;
              game.modding.terminal.echo("SUBSPACE DETECTED:  " + event.ship.name);
            }
            break;
        }

        case "ship_spawned": {
            ensureCustom(event.ship);

            checkBanned(event.ship);
            if (event.ship.custom.hasBeenKicked) return;

            if (!event.ship.custom.passedPin) {
                PinUI.show(event.ship);
                return;
            }

            if (!event.ship.custom.init) initShip(event.ship);
            else respawnShip(event.ship);

            break;
        }
        case "ship_destroyed": {
            shipDestroyed(event.ship, event.killer);
            break;
        }
        case "ship_disconnected": {
            const shipName = getPlayerName(event.ship);

            if (!gameOptions.BannedList.includes(shipName)) {
                if (event.ship.custom && event.ship.custom.ISidle && !event.ship.custom.hasBeenKicked) {
                    ensureCustom(event.ship);
                    event.ship.custom.hasBeenKicked = true;
                    const shipIndex = game.ships.findIndex(s => s === event.ship);
                    ban(shipIndex, "Left while frozen");
                    return;
                }
                game.modding.terminal.echo(`\n[[g;#ff8770;]Player ${shipName} has left the game.]`);
            }
            break;
        }

        case "team_win": {
            event.ships.forEach(ship => {
                alert(ship, "Congrats!", "Your team won!", "rgba(255,176,0,0.95)", 3000);
            });

            try {
                const winningTeamHue = game.options.teams[event.ships[0].team].hue;
                const teamName = (winningTeamHue === 240) ? "BLUE" : (winningTeamHue === 0) ? "RED" : String(event.ships[0].team);
                game.modding.terminal.echo(`\n[[g;#ffb000;]Team ${teamName} won the game!]`);
            } catch (e) {
                game.modding.terminal.echo(`\n[[g;#ffb000;]A team won the game!]`);
            }
            break;
        }
    }
};

function shipDestroyed(ship, killer) {
  if (gameOptions.RespawnTimers.enabled && gameOptions.RespawnTimers.active) {
    startRespawnTimer(ship);
  }
}
